# gui/main_window.py
import os
import webbrowser
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import messagebox

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import LOG_FOLDER
from core.qb_api import detect_qbittorrent_port, is_qb_running, launch_qbittorrent, close_qbittorrent
from core.autostart import set_autostart, is_autostart_enabled
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from gui.tray import SystemTrayController


class TorrentApp:
    def __init__(self, root, on_quit_callback=None):
        self.root = root
        self.on_quit_callback = on_quit_callback
        self.qb_ip, self.qb_port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
        self.port = cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000)
        self._closing = False

        self.root.title("tbr PRO — Control Panel")
        self.root.geometry("480x540")
        self.root.minsize(440, 510)
        self.root.configure(bg="#121214")

        summary_tracker.start_auto_export(get_global_torrent_summaries)
        self.build_ui()

        self.tray = SystemTrayController(
            root=self.root,
            on_show_window=self.restore_from_tray,
            on_open_web=self.open_web,
            on_exit_app=self.quit_app
        )
        self.tray_active = self.tray.start()

        self.root.bind("<Unmap>", self._on_unmap)
        self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.quit_app)

        if self.tray_active and cfg.getboolean('Start_Minimized', False):
            self.root.withdraw()

        self.update_status()

    def build_ui(self):
        f = tk.Frame(self.root, bg="#1a1a20", bd=1, relief="solid")
        f.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=14, pady=14)

        tk.Label(f, text="⚡ tbr PRO CONTROL PANEL", font=("Segoe UI", 13, "bold"), fg="#2ecc71", bg="#1a1a20").pack(
            pady=(14, 4))
        tk.Label(f, text="Веб-сервер работает в фоне", font=("Segoe UI", 9), fg="#95a5a6", bg="#1a1a20").pack(
            pady=(0, 12))

        tk.Button(f, text="🌐 ОТКРЫТЬ ВЕБ-ИНТЕРФЕЙС", font=("Segoe UI", 11, "bold"), bd=0,
                  bg="#2ecc71", fg="#000000", cursor="hand2", command=self.open_web).pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=4,
                                                                                          ipady=6)

        net_frame = tk.Frame(f, bg="#262630", bd=0)
        net_frame.pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=12, ipady=6)

        tk.Label(net_frame, text="Хост и пароль веб-доступа (для 0.0.0.0 пароль обязателен):",
                 font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), fg="#95a5a6", bg="#262630").pack(anchor="w", padx=10, pady=(6, 2))

        row_net = tk.Frame(net_frame, bg="#262630")
        row_net.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=2)

        self.e_host = tk.Entry(row_net, width=12, font=("Segoe UI", 9), bg="#1a1a20", fg="#ffffff",
                               insertbackground="#ffffff", bd=0)
        self.e_host.insert(0, cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1'))
        self.e_host.pack(side="left", ipady=3, padx=(0, 6))

        self.e_pass = tk.Entry(row_net, show="*", font=("Segoe UI", 9), bg="#1a1a20", fg="#ffffff",
                               insertbackground="#ffffff", bd=0)
        self.e_pass.insert(0, cfg.get('Web_Password', ''))
        self.e_pass.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, ipady=3, padx=(0, 6))

        tk.Button(row_net, text="💾", font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), bg="#2ecc71", fg="#000000", bd=0, padx=8,
                  cursor="hand2", command=self.save_net_settings).pack(side="right")

        self.lbl_qb = tk.Label(f, text="qBittorrent: проверка...", font=("Segoe UI", 9, "bold"), fg="#ffffff",
                               bg="#1a1a20")
        self.lbl_qb.pack(pady=(6, 4))

        qb_row = tk.Frame(f, bg="#1a1a20")
        qb_row.pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=4)
        qb_row.columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1)

        self.btn_qb_toggle = tk.Button(qb_row, text="Пуск / Стоп qB", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630",
                                       fg="#ffffff", cursor="hand2", command=self.toggle_qb)
        self.btn_qb_toggle.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=3, ipady=4, sticky="ew")

        tk.Button(qb_row, text="🔄 Перезапуск qB", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630", fg="#ffffff",
                  cursor="hand2", command=self.restart_qb).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=3, ipady=4, sticky="ew")

        bot_row = tk.Frame(f, bg="#1a1a20")
        bot_row.pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=(12, 4))
        bot_row.columnconfigure((0, 1), weight=1)

        self.btn_auto = tk.Button(bot_row, text="Автозагрузка", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630",
                                  fg="#ffffff", cursor="hand2", command=self.toggle_autostart)
        self.btn_auto.grid(row=0, column=0, padx=3, ipady=4, sticky="ew")

        tk.Button(bot_row, text="📁 Папка логов", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630", fg="#ffffff",
                  cursor="hand2", command=lambda: os.startfile(LOG_FOLDER)).grid(row=0, column=1, padx=3, ipady=4,
                                                                                 sticky="ew")

        self.var_start_min = tk.BooleanVar(value=cfg.getboolean('Start_Minimized', False))
        tk.Checkbutton(
            f, text="Запускать свёрнутым в трей", variable=self.var_start_min,
            command=self.toggle_start_minimized, font=("Segoe UI", 9),
            bg="#1a1a20", fg="#95a5a6", selectcolor="#262630",
            activebackground="#1a1a20", activeforeground="#ffffff", bd=0
        ).pack(pady=(6, 4))

        tk.Button(f, text="🔑 Перевыпустить Admin-токен", font=("Segoe UI", 8, "bold"), bd=0, bg="#262630",
                  fg="#f39c12", cursor="hand2", command=self.reset_admin_token).pack(fill="x", padx=24, pady=(4, 0),
                                                                                     ipady=5)

    def _on_unmap(self, event):
        if event.widget is self.root and self.tray_active and not self._closing:
            try:
                if self.root.state() == 'iconic':
                    self.root.withdraw()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def restore_from_tray(self):
        try:
            self.root.deiconify()
            self.root.state('normal')
            self.root.lift()
            self.root.focus_force()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def quit_app(self):
        if self._closing:
            return
        self._closing = True
        if self.tray:
            self.tray.stop()
        if self.on_quit_callback:
            try:
                self.on_quit_callback()
            except Exception:
                pass
        try:
            self.root.quit()
            self.root.destroy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def toggle_start_minimized(self):
        cfg.set('Start_Minimized', str(bool(self.var_start_min.get())))
        cfg.save()

    def reset_admin_token(self):
        new_tok = profiles_db.reset_admin_token_local()
        if new_tok:
            self.root.clipboard_clear()
            self.root.clipboard_append(new_tok)
            messagebox.showinfo("Admin-токен", f"Новый Admin-токен скопирован в буфер обмена:\n\n{new_tok}")

    def save_net_settings(self):
        host = self.e_host.get().strip() or '127.0.0.1'
        pwd = self.e_pass.get().strip()
        if host not in ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost') and not pwd:
            return messagebox.showwarning("Внимание", "Для хоста 0.0.0.0 обязательно впишите пароль!")
        cfg.set('Web_Host', host)
        cfg.set('Web_Password', pwd)
        cfg.save()
        messagebox.showinfo("Сохранено", "Настройки сохранены!\nПерезапустите tbr, чтобы применить новый хост.")

    def open_web(self):
        port = cfg.getint('Web_Port', self.port)
        webbrowser.open(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/")

    def update_status(self):
        if self._closing:
            return
        running = is_qb_running()
        if running:
            self.lbl_qb.config(text="qBittorrent: 🟢 Запущен", fg="#2ecc71")
            self.btn_qb_toggle.config(text="⏹ Остановить qB", bg="#e74c3c", fg="#ffffff")
        else:
            self.lbl_qb.config(text="qBittorrent: 🔴 Остановлен", fg="#e74c3c")
            self.btn_qb_toggle.config(text="▶ Запустить qB", bg="#2ecc71", fg="#000000")

        auto_on = is_autostart_enabled()
        self.btn_auto.config(text="Автозагрузка: ВКЛ" if auto_on else "Автозагрузка: ВЫКЛ",
                             fg="#2ecc71" if auto_on else "#95a5a6")
        self.root.after(3000, self.update_status)

    def toggle_qb(self):
        if is_qb_running():
            close_qbittorrent()
        else:
            launch_qbittorrent()
        self.root.after(1000, self.update_status)

    def restart_qb(self):
        close_qbittorrent()
        self.root.after(1500, launch_qbittorrent)
        self.root.after(2500, self.update_status)

    def toggle_autostart(self):
        curr = is_autostart_enabled()
        ok, msg = set_autostart(not curr)
        messagebox.showinfo("Автозагрузка", msg)
        self.update_status()

# gui/theme.py
def get_theme_config(is_dark_theme):
    themes = {
        True: {
            "bm": "#121214", "bp": "#1a1a20", "bi": "#2d2d38", "bt": "#1e1e24", "bo": "#0c0c0f",
            "ft": "#ffffff", "fl": "#95a5a6", "fo": "#2ecc71", "ib": "white", "tc": "#2a2a32",
            "cb": "#2ecc71", "rb": "#3498db", "tb": "#2a2a32", "cpb": "#34495e", "sh": "#34495e"
        },
        False: {
            "bm": "#f0f2f5", "bp": "#ffffff", "bi": "#e4e6eb", "bt": "#ffffff", "bo": "#f5f6f7",
            "ft": "#000000", "fl": "#4b4f56", "fo": "#000000", "ib": "black", "tc": "#e4e6eb",
            "cb": "#0078d4", "rb": "#e1dfdd", "tb": "#e1dfdd", "cpb": "#e1dfdd", "sh": "#c8c6c4"
        }
    }
    return themes[is_dark_theme]

# gui/tray.py
import os
import threading
from utils.helpers import write_error_log

try:
    import pystray
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
    TRAY_LIBS_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    TRAY_LIBS_AVAILABLE = False


def has_display():
    if os.name == 'nt':
        return True
    return bool(os.environ.get('DISPLAY') or os.environ.get('WAYLAND_DISPLAY'))


def _create_tray_image():
    size = 64
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    draw.rounded_rectangle([2, 2, size - 2, size - 2], radius=14, fill=(26, 26, 32, 255), outline=(46, 204, 113, 255), width=3)
    # Молния в стиле логотипа tbr PRO
    bolt = [(35, 10), (17, 35), (31, 35), (27, 54), (47, 29), (33, 29)]
    draw.polygon(bolt, fill=(46, 204, 113, 255))
    return img


class SystemTrayController:
    def __init__(self, root, on_show_window, on_open_web, on_exit_app):
        self.root = root
        self.on_show_window = on_show_window
        self.on_open_web = on_open_web
        self.on_exit_app = on_exit_app
        self.icon = None
        self.is_active = False

    def start(self):
        if not TRAY_LIBS_AVAILABLE or not has_display():
            return False

        try:
            menu = pystray.Menu(
                pystray.MenuItem("Открыть окно", self._dispatch(self.on_show_window), default=True),
                pystray.MenuItem("Открыть веб-панель", self._dispatch(self.on_open_web)),
                pystray.Menu.SEPARATOR,
                pystray.MenuItem("Выход", self._dispatch(self.on_exit_app))
            )
            self.icon = pystray.Icon("tbr_pro", _create_tray_image(), "tbr PRO", menu)
            threading.Thread(target=self.icon.run, daemon=True).start()
            self.is_active = True
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("Ошибка запуска системного трея", e)
            self.is_active = False
            return False

    def _dispatch(self, callback):
        def handler(icon, item):
            try:
                self.root.after(0, callback)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return handler

    def stop(self):
        self.is_active = False
        if self.icon:
            try:
                self.icon.stop()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.icon = None

# main.py
import sys
import os
import atexit
import signal
import argparse
import webbrowser
import threading

from core.backup_manager import apply_pending_restore_if_any
apply_pending_restore_if_any()

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_log
from core.single_instance import SingleInstanceLock
from web.app import web_server
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker


def parse_arguments():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="tbr v2.0 (Швейцарский нож qBittorrent)")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=None, help="Порт веб-сервера")
    parser.add_argument("--host", type=str, default=None, help="Хост веб-сервера")
    parser.add_argument("--web-only", "--headless", dest="web_only", action="store_true",
                        help="Запустить только веб без окна (для серверов и Docker)")
    parser.add_argument("--no-web", action="store_true", help="Запустить только окно без веб-сервера")
    return parser.parse_args()


def get_browser_url(host, port):
    b_host = '127.0.0.1' if host in ('0.0.0.0', '*') else host
    return f"http://{b_host}:{port}"


def main():
    args = parse_arguments()

    instance_lock = SingleInstanceLock()
    if not instance_lock.acquire(wait_sec=1.5):
        msg = "tbr уже запущен (проверь системный трей)"
        if args.web_only:
            write_log("app", msg)
            print(f"⚠️ {msg}")
            sys.exit(0)
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            from tkinter import messagebox
            tmp_root = tk.Tk()
            tmp_root.withdraw()
            messagebox.showinfo("tbr PRO", msg)
            tmp_root.destroy()
        except Exception:
            print(f"⚠️ {msg}")
        sys.exit(0)

    env_pwd = os.environ.get('TBR_WEB_PASSWORD', '').strip()
    if env_pwd:
        cfg.set('Web_Password', env_pwd)
    if args.port:
        cfg.set('Web_Port', args.port)
    if args.host:
        cfg.set('Web_Host', args.host)
    if args.port or args.host or env_pwd:
        cfg.save()

    web_port = cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000)
    web_host = cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1')

    def on_shutdown():
        summary_tracker.flush_on_exit()
        web_server.stop()
        instance_lock.release()

    atexit.register(on_shutdown)
    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
        try:
            signal.signal(sig, lambda s, f: (on_shutdown(), sys.exit(0)))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not args.no_web:
        web_server.start(host=web_host, port=web_port)

    if args.web_only:
        url = get_browser_url(web_host, web_port)
        if os.name == 'nt':
            try:
                webbrowser.open(url)
            except Exception:
                pass
        print(f"✅ Сервер запущен на {url}. Нажмите Ctrl+C для выхода.")
        try:
            while True:
                threading.Event().wait(1)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            on_shutdown()
            sys.exit(0)

    import tkinter as tk
    from tkinter import messagebox
    try:
        from gui.main_window import TorrentApp
    except ImportError:
        TorrentApp = None

    if TorrentApp is None:
        messagebox.showerror("Ошибка запуска", "Файл интерфейса gui/main_window.py не найден!")
        on_shutdown()
        return

    if os.name == 'nt':
        try:
            from ctypes import windll
            windll.shcore.SetProcessDpiAwareness(1)
        except Exception:
            pass

    root = tk.Tk()
    TorrentApp(root, on_quit_callback=on_shutdown)
    root.mainloop()
    on_shutdown()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

> [читать на русском](README_RU.md)

# tbr pro — qbittorrent swiss-army knife

> supports **en / ru** ui toggle right in the header!

# features

1. **pause and resume all torrents at once** (groundbreaking technology, i know)

2. **export csv summaries** so you can finally show your wife, mom, dad, or boyfriend why your pc hasn't been turned off in a month

3. **fastresume stats merger with safe source cleanup:** if you're a ratio-obsessed stats nerd and don't want to lose all upload stats on a torrent—or keep 50 versions of the same game on your pc—you can transfer `uploaded` & `seeding_time` metadata to a new torrent using my *innovative* merger (safely closes and restarts qBittorrent so bencode doesn't get corrupted). Now with optional automated source deletion: it checks file paths to prevent accidental data loss and safely downgrades to torrent-only removal if folders overlap

4. **smart eta forecaster & honest speed history:** calculates when you'll hit a specific upload/ratio goal using weighted historical data (SQLite). The load chart is now continuous—no dumb hourly buckets, smoothed phantom spikes from newly added torrents, and detailed tooltips (hover over any point to see exact transfer speed and volume uploaded between snapshots)

5. **dead torrents & tracker error scanner:** multi-threaded scan through all trackers across your library, groups recurring tracker errors, and lets you pause all dead weight with a single click

6. **android home screen widget (`tbr-widget`) + PWA:** yes, you can now stare at your seedbox speeds right from your phone's home screen. Native Android widget with one-tap chart toggling (24h speed vs daily bars) and a manual `↻` refresh button, plus the web UI installs cleanly as an address-bar-free PWA

7. **profiles & RBAC (Admin / Viewer):** generate 1-click magic links with read-only Viewer permissions for friends or family—they get to admire your upload flex without being able to stop torrents or mess up your config

8. **personal spam bot disguised as automation (tg & discord):** set rules (min ratio / seeding days / upload volume / average speed in window W) so torrents auto-pause or get tagged—or just drop a discord webhook into your friends' server and forcibly subject them to your upload achievements

9. **works even without qbittorrent webui running** (well, except tab 1—obviously, it basically mirrors the webui)

10. **light as a feather** — tiny standalone `.exe`, no python required

11. **clean (for me) modular architecture** — every file strictly under 300 lines, zero spaghetti code

# what do i need to make it work?

1. **qbittorrent webui** — password protected setups are supported! just enter your qB webui login/password in `⚙️ settings` (or leave it bypassed on localhost, up to you)

2. **qbittorrent** — haven't tested other clients (transmission doesn't count, it's kinda creepy). and if you don't use qB, why are you even here, normie?

3. **windows / linux / docker** — `.exe` is built for windows (tested on win 11), and now it also runs headless on linux & docker (`python main.py --headless` or via `Dockerfile`)

4. **want the phone widget?** grab the prebuilt `.apk` from the companion repo [tbr-widget](https://github.com/kamushek0o/tbr-widget/releases) — plug in your server URL and Viewer token, and you're good to go

5. **paranoid about random `.exe` files?** fair enough! the `.exe` in releases is auto-compiled by **github actions** straight from this open-source code (no sketchy local builds). or just run `pip install -r requirements.txt` -> `python main.py`, or build it yourself via `build.bat`

# tbr pro — швейцарский нож для qbittorrent

> встроенный переключатель языка **en / ru** прямо в шапке!

# фичи

1. **останавливать и запускать все торренты разом** (нихуя себе, я считаю)

2. **выгружать сводки в csv**, чтобы вы наконец-то смогли показать жене, маме, отцу или парню, почему у вас комп не выключается уже месяц

3. **подменщик статистики (.fastresume) с умным удалением исходника:** если вы любитель подрочить на статистику и не хотите терять весь аплоад с раздачи — или держать на своём пк 50 версий одной игры — вы можете перенести метаданные `uploaded` и `seeding_time` в новый торрент через мой *инновационный* подменитель (он сам безопасно гасит и поднимает qBittorrent, чтобы бенкод не побился). А теперь ещё и умеет сам подчищать старый торрент: сверяет пути файлов, чтобы случайно не снести общий контент, и понижает удаление до «только запись в клиенте», если папки пересекаются

4. **умный калькулятор прогнозов и честная история отдачи:** считает, когда именно вы добьёте нужный аплоад или ratio по взвешенной истории (SQLite). График нагрузки теперь непрерывный — без тупых часовых бакетов, со сглаживанием фантомных всплесков и детальными тултипами (наводишь на точку — видишь точную скорость и сколько именно налило между замерами)

5. **сканер мёртвых раздач и дохлых трекеров:** в многопотоке опрашивает все трекеры в клиенте, группирует ошибки и даёт одной кнопкой отправить в нокаут (на паузу) весь мусор, который годами висит и спамит логами

6. **виджет на рабочий стол Android (`tbr-widget`) + PWA:** да-да, теперь можно залипать на скорость сидбокса прямо с экрана телефона. Есть нативный виджет с переключением графиков в один тап (скорость за 24ч / столбики по дням) и кнопкой `↻`, а веб-морда ставится как PWA-приложение без адресной строки

7. **профили и права доступа (Admin / Viewer):** можно выдать другу или маме безопасную ссылку в один клик с правами Viewer — смотреть на ваши терабайты смогут, а вот случайно стопнуть раздачи или снести конфиг уже хуй

8. **спам-бот под видом автоматизации (тг и дискорд):** задаёте правила (мин. ratio / дни сидирования / порог отдачи / средняя скорость в окне W), чтобы торренты сами стопались или получали теги — ну или просто закидываете вебхук в дискорд к друзьям и насильственно заставляете их любоваться вашими рекордами

9. **может работать и без запущенной вебморды кубита** (кроме 1 вкладки, не, ну а чо хотели, она вебморду по сути и повторяет)

10. **лёгкая, как пушинка** — маленький автономный `.exe`, python не нужен

11. **удобная (для меня) архитектура** — все модули строго до 300 строк, никакого спагетти-говна

# utils/bencode.py

def decode_bencode(data):
    """Декодирует bencode-данные с автоматическим декодированием ключей и текста в UTF-8."""
    if isinstance(data, str):
        data = data.encode('latin1')

    def decode_first(idx):
        char = data[idx:idx + 1]
        if char == b'i':
            end = data.index(b'e', idx + 1)
            return int(data[idx + 1:end]), end + 1
        elif char == b'l':
            idx += 1
            res = []
            while data[idx:idx + 1] != b'e':
                val, idx = decode_first(idx)
                res.append(val)
            return res, idx + 1
        elif char == b'd':
            idx += 1
            res = {}
            while data[idx:idx + 1] != b'e':
                raw_key, idx = decode_first(idx)
                # Ключи словаря всегда декодируем в строковый вид (str)
                if isinstance(raw_key, bytes):
                    try:
                        key = raw_key.decode('utf-8')
                    except UnicodeDecodeError:
                        key = raw_key.decode('latin1', errors='replace')
                else:
                    key = str(raw_key)

                val, idx = decode_first(idx)
                res[key] = val
            return res, idx + 1
        elif char.isdigit():
            colon = data.index(b':', idx)
            length = int(data[idx:colon])
            start = colon + 1
            end = start + length
            raw_bytes = data[start:end]

            # Текстовые строки декодируем в str, бинарные данные (куски, хэши) оставляем в bytes
            try:
                val = raw_bytes.decode('utf-8')
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                val = raw_bytes
            return val, end
        raise ValueError(f"Неизвестный токен bencode на позиции {idx}: {char}")

    val, _ = decode_first(0)
    return val


def encode_bencode(val):
    """Безопасное кодирование обратно в bencode с корректной сортировкой ключей."""
    if isinstance(val, int):
        return b'i' + str(val).encode('ascii') + b'e'
    elif isinstance(val, (bytes, bytearray)):
        return str(len(val)).encode('ascii') + b':' + bytes(val)
    elif isinstance(val, str):
        b = val.encode('utf-8')
        return str(len(b)).encode('ascii') + b':' + b
    elif isinstance(val, (list, tuple)):
        return b'l' + b''.join(encode_bencode(x) for x in val) + b'e'
    elif isinstance(val, dict):
        def to_bytes(k):
            return k if isinstance(k, (bytes, bytearray)) else str(k).encode('utf-8')

        sorted_keys = sorted(val.keys(), key=to_bytes)
        out = [b'd']
        for k in sorted_keys:
            out.append(encode_bencode(to_bytes(k)))
            out.append(encode_bencode(val[k]))
        out.append(b'e')
        return b''.join(out)
    raise TypeError(f"Невозможно закодировать тип в bencode: {type(val)}")

# utils/config.py
import os
import sys
import shutil
import configparser
from utils.helpers import APP_DATA_DIR

if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
    OLD_BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
else:
    OLD_BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

CONFIG_FILE = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'config.ini')
OLD_CONFIG_FILE = os.path.join(OLD_BASE_DIR, 'config.ini')


class AppConfig:
    def __init__(self):
        self.config_file = CONFIG_FILE
        self.config = configparser.ConfigParser()
        self._migrate_old_config()
        self.load()

    def _migrate_old_config(self):
        if not os.path.exists(self.config_file) and os.path.exists(OLD_CONFIG_FILE):
            try:
                os.makedirs(APP_DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
                shutil.move(OLD_CONFIG_FILE, self.config_file)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    shutil.copy2(OLD_CONFIG_FILE, self.config_file)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    def load(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.config_file):
            try:
                self.config.read(self.config_file, encoding='utf-8')
            except Exception:
                pass
        if 'Settings' not in self.config:
            self.config['Settings'] = {}

        defaults = {
            'Theme': 'True',
            'Web_Port': '5000',
            'Web_Host': '127.0.0.1',
            'Web_Password': '',
            'AutoStartWeb': 'True',
            'AutoOpenBrowser': 'False',
            'WebUI_Address': '',
            'WebUI_Port': '',
            'WebUI_User': 'admin',
            'WebUI_Password': '',
            'Weight_W1': '70',
            'Weight_W2': '30',
            'Weight_W3': '0',
            'Snapshot_Interval_Min': '60',
            'Start_Minimized': 'False',
            'Auto_Enabled': 'False',
            'Auto_Interval_Min': '5',
            'TG_Bot_Token': '',
            'TG_Chat_ID': '',
            'Discord_Webhook': ''
        }
        changed = False
        for k, v in defaults.items():
            if k not in self.config['Settings']:
                self.config['Settings'][k] = v
                changed = True
        if changed or not os.path.exists(self.config_file):
            self.save()

    def save(self):
        try:
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.config_file), exist_ok=True)
            with open(self.config_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                self.config.write(f)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def get(self, key, fallback=''):
        return self.config.get('Settings', key, fallback=fallback)

    def getint(self, key, fallback=0):
        try:
            val = self.config.get('Settings', key, fallback='').strip()
            return int(val) if val else fallback
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return fallback

    def getboolean(self, key, fallback=False):
        try:
            return self.config.getboolean('Settings', key, fallback=fallback)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return fallback

    def set(self, key, value):
        if 'Settings' not in self.config:
            self.config['Settings'] = {}
        self.config['Settings'][key] = str(value)


cfg = AppConfig()

# utils/helpers.py
import os
import datetime
import traceback
import re


def _get_app_data_dir():
    env_dir = os.environ.get('TBR_DATA_DIR', '').strip()
    if env_dir:
        return env_dir
    if os.name == 'nt':
        local_app = os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA', os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'AppData', 'Local'))
        return os.path.join(local_app, 'tbr')
    return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), '.config', 'tbr')


def _get_bt_backup_path():
    env_bt = os.environ.get('TBR_BT_BACKUP', '').strip()
    if env_bt:
        return env_bt
    if os.name == 'nt':
        return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), 'AppData', 'Local', 'qBittorrent', 'BT_backup')
    return os.path.join(os.path.expanduser('~'), '.local', 'share', 'qBittorrent', 'BT_backup')


APP_DATA_DIR = _get_app_data_dir()
BT_BACKUP_PATH = _get_bt_backup_path()
LOG_FOLDER = os.path.join(APP_DATA_DIR, 'logs')

os.makedirs(APP_DATA_DIR, exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs(LOG_FOLDER, exist_ok=True)


def write_log(category, message):
    try:
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
        log_file = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, f"{category}.log")
        with open(log_file, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(f"[{ts}] {message}\n")
    except Exception:
        pass


def write_error_log(context, error):
    try:
        ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
        log_file = os.path.join(LOG_FOLDER, "error.log")
        with open(log_file, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(f"[{ts}] ОШИБКА в {context}: {error}\n")
            f.write(traceback.format_exc() + "\n")
    except Exception:
        pass


def sanitize_csv_cell(val):
    """Защита от CSV Formula Injection."""
    s = str(val) if val is not None else ''
    if s and s[0] in ('=', '+', '-', '@', '\t', '\r'):
        return "'" + s
    return s


def format_file_size(size_bytes):
    if size_bytes is None:
        return "0 B"
    try:
        size = float(size_bytes)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return "0 B"
    if size <= 0:
        return "0 B"
    if size < 1.0:
        return f"{size:.2f} B"
    for unit in ['B', 'KiB', 'MiB', 'GiB', 'TiB', 'PiB']:
        if size < 1024.0:
            return f"{size:.2f} {unit}"
        size /= 1024.0
    return f"{size:.2f} EiB"


def parse_bytes(text, torrent_size=0):
    if not text:
        raise ValueError("Цель не может быть пустой!")
    text = text.strip().lower().replace(',', '.')

    m_ratio = re.match(r'^([\d\.]+)\s*(?:x|х|ratio|р|ратио)?$', text)
    if m_ratio:
        ratio = float(m_ratio.group(1))
        if ratio <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Ratio должен быть больше 0!")
        return int(torrent_size * ratio)

    m_size = re.match(r'^([\d\.]+)\s*([a-zа-яё]+)?$', text)
    if not m_size:
        raise ValueError("Неверный формат цели! (Пример: 50 тиб, 100 GiB или 2.0x)")

    val = float(m_size.group(1))
    unit = (m_size.group(2) or 'b').lower()

    multipliers = {
        'b': 1, 'б': 1, 'байт': 1,
        'k': 1024, 'kb': 1024, 'kib': 1024, 'к': 1024, 'кб': 1024, 'киб': 1024,
        'm': 1024**2, 'mb': 1024**2, 'mib': 1024**2, 'м': 1024**2, 'мб': 1024**2, 'миб': 1024**2,
        'g': 1024**3, 'gb': 1024**3, 'gib': 1024**3, 'г': 1024**3, 'гб': 1024**3, 'гиб': 1024**3,
        't': 1024**4, 'tb': 1024**4, 'tib': 1024**4, 'т': 1024**4, 'тб': 1024**4, 'тиб': 1024**4,
        'p': 1024**5, 'pb': 1024**5, 'pib': 1024**5, 'п': 1024**5, 'пб': 1024**5, 'пиб': 1024**5
    }

    if unit not in multipliers:
        raise ValueError(f"Неизвестная единица измерения: {unit}")

    return int(val * multipliers[unit])


def parse_speed(text, default_bytes_sec=0):
    if not text or text.strip().lower() in ('авто', 'auto'):
        return default_bytes_sec
    text = text.strip().lower().replace(',', '.')

    m = re.match(r'^([\d\.]+)\s*([a-zа-яё/]+)?$', text)
    if not m:
        raise ValueError("Неверный формат скорости!")

    val = float(m.group(1))
    raw_unit = (m.group(2) or 'mib').replace('/s', '').replace('/с', '').replace('/сек', '').strip()

    multipliers = {
        'b': 1, 'б': 1,
        'k': 1024, 'kb': 1024, 'kib': 1024, 'к': 1024, 'кб': 1024, 'киб': 1024,
        'm': 1024**2, 'mb': 1024**2, 'mib': 1024**2, 'м': 1024**2, 'мб': 1024**2, 'миб': 1024**2,
        'g': 1024**3, 'gb': 1024**3, 'gib': 1024**3, 'г': 1024**3, 'гб': 1024**3, 'гиб': 1024**3
    }

    unit = raw_unit if raw_unit in multipliers else 'mib'
    return int(val * multipliers[unit])

# web/app.py
import os
import sys
import time
import threading
from flask import Flask
from werkzeug.exceptions import HTTPException

try:
    import waitress
    from waitress import create_server
    HAS_WAITRESS = True
except ImportError:
    waitress = None
    create_server = None
    HAS_WAITRESS = False

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import write_error_log
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.automation import automation_engine
from web.security import register_security
from web.routes.torrents_bp import torrents_bp
from web.routes.analytics_bp import analytics_bp
from web.routes.automation_bp import automation_bp
from web.routes.settings_bp import settings_bp
from web.routes.profiles_bp import profiles_bp
from web.routes.widget_bp import widget_bp


def get_resource_path(relative_path):
    base_path = getattr(sys, '_MEIPASS', os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
    return os.path.join(base_path, relative_path)


app = Flask(
    __name__,
    template_folder=get_resource_path(os.path.join('web', 'templates')),
    static_folder=get_resource_path(os.path.join('web', 'static'))
)
app.secret_key = os.urandom(24)
app.config['BUILD_VER'] = str(int(time.time()))
app.config['SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY'] = True
app.config['SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE'] = 'Lax'


@app.errorhandler(Exception)
def handle_unhandled_exception(e):
    if isinstance(e, HTTPException):
        return e
    write_error_log("Flask HTTP 500", e)
    return "Internal Server Error", 500


register_security(app)
app.register_blueprint(torrents_bp)
app.register_blueprint(analytics_bp)
app.register_blueprint(automation_bp)
app.register_blueprint(settings_bp)
app.register_blueprint(profiles_bp)
app.register_blueprint(widget_bp)


class WebServerController:
    def __init__(self):
        self.server_instance = None
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self.current_host = '127.0.0.1'
        self.current_port = 5000

    def start(self, host=None, port=None):
        with self._lock:
            self._start_locked(host, port)

    def _start_locked(self, host=None, port=None):
        h = (host or cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1')).strip() or '127.0.0.1'
        p = port or cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000)

        is_exposed = h not in ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost')
        if is_exposed and not cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip():
            print("⚠️ Внимание: выбран внешний хост, но пароль не задан! Безопасный откат на 127.0.0.1.")
            h = '127.0.0.1'
            is_exposed = False

        app.config['IS_EXPOSED'] = is_exposed
        self.current_host = h
        self.current_port = p

        summary_tracker.start_auto_export(get_global_torrent_summaries)
        automation_engine.start()

        try:
            if HAS_WAITRESS and create_server:
                self.server_instance = create_server(app, host=h, port=p, threads=8)
                engine_name = "Waitress"
            else:
                from werkzeug.serving import make_server
                self.server_instance = make_server(h, p, app, threaded=True)
                engine_name = "Werkzeug (fallback)"

            print(f"🚀 Сервер {engine_name} запущен на http://{h}:{p} (Авторизация: {'ВКЛ' if is_exposed else 'ВЫКЛ'})")

            def serve():
                try:
                    if self.server_instance:
                        if hasattr(self.server_instance, 'run'):
                            self.server_instance.run()
                        else:
                            self.server_instance.serve_forever()
                except Exception as e:
                    write_error_log("WebServer serve error", e)

            threading.Thread(target=serve, daemon=True).start()
        except Exception as e:
            write_error_log("WebServerController Start Error", e)
            print(f"❌ Ошибка запуска веб-сервера на {h}:{p}: {e}")

    def stop(self):
        with self._lock:
            if self.server_instance:
                try:
                    if hasattr(self.server_instance, 'close'):
                        self.server_instance.close()
                    elif hasattr(self.server_instance, 'shutdown'):
                        self.server_instance.shutdown()
                except Exception:
                    pass
                self.server_instance = None

    def restart(self, new_host, new_port):
        def do_restart():
            time.sleep(0.5)
            self.stop()
            with self._lock:
                self._start_locked(new_host, new_port)

        threading.Thread(target=do_restart, daemon=True).start()


web_server = WebServerController()
app.config['WEB_SERVER'] = web_server

# web/routes/analytics_bp.py
import os
import io
import csv
import datetime
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g

from utils.helpers import sanitize_csv_cell, format_file_size, LOG_FOLDER
from core.torrent_parser import get_global_torrent_summaries
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from web.security import HASH_RE

analytics_bp = Blueprint('analytics_bp', __name__)


def _parse_day_bounds(date_str):
    try:
        d = datetime.datetime.strptime(str(date_str).strip(), '%Y-%m-%d')
    except ValueError:
        d = datetime.datetime.now().replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
    start_ts = d.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0).timestamp()
    end_ts = d.replace(hour=23, minute=59, second=59, microsecond=999999).timestamp()
    return d.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'), start_ts, end_ts


def _is_strict_localhost():
    if os.path.exists('/.dockerenv') or bool(os.environ.get('TBR_DATA_DIR', '').strip()):
        return False
    if request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.headers.get('X-Real-IP'):
        return False
    return (request.remote_addr or '').strip() in ('127.0.0.1', '::1')


@analytics_bp.route('/api/summary')
def api_summary():
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.get_stats(summaries))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/history')
def api_analytics_history():
    t_hash = request.args.get('hash', 'all')
    if t_hash != 'all' and not HASH_RE.fullmatch(t_hash):
        return jsonify([])
    limit = min(100, max(1, request.args.get('limit', 36, type=int)))
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.get_torrent_hourly_history(t_hash, limit_hours=limit))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/analytics/charts')
def api_analytics_charts():
    hours = request.args.get('hours', 24, type=int)
    top_n = request.args.get('top_n', 5, type=int)
    top_period = request.args.get('top_period', '1d')
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.get_charts_data(summaries, hours=hours, top_n=top_n, top_period=top_period))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/smart_calc', methods=['POST'])
def api_smart_calc():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неверный формат JSON'}), 400
    summaries = get_global_torrent_summaries()
    return jsonify(summary_tracker.calculate_smart_forecast(
        str(data.get('target_type', 'torrent')), str(data.get('hash', '')),
        str(data.get('target', ''))[:64], str(data.get('speed', ''))[:64], summaries
    ))


@analytics_bp.route('/api/smart_calc/export', methods=['POST'])
def api_smart_calc_export():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Ожидался JSON объект'}), 400
    forecast_data = data.get('forecast_data')
    if not isinstance(forecast_data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Отсутствует forecast_data'}), 400
    rows = forecast_data.get('forecast_rows')
    if not isinstance(rows, list) or len(rows) > 100 or not all(isinstance(r, dict) for r in rows):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные строки прогноза'}), 400
    filepath, filename = summary_tracker.export_forecast_csv(forecast_data)
    if not filepath:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Ошибка создания файла'}), 500
    with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
        file_bytes = f.read()
    return Response(file_bytes, mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename*=UTF-8''{urllib.parse.quote(filename)}"})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/export_csv', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_export_csv():
    if request.method == 'POST':
        req_data = request.get_json(silent=True)
        if not isinstance(req_data, dict):
            return jsonify({'error': 'Некорректный JSON'}), 400
        items = req_data.get('items')
        if not isinstance(items, list) or len(items) > 5000 or not all(isinstance(x, dict) for x in items):
            return jsonify({'error': 'Некорректный список или превышен лимит 5000 строк'}), 400
    else:
        items = summary_tracker.get_stats(get_global_torrent_summaries())['items']

    summary_tracker.save_manual_csv(items)
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output, delimiter=';')
    writer.writerow(['Имя', 'Хэш', 'Размер (байт)', 'Размер (ГБ)', 'Отдано (байт)', 'Отдано (ГБ)', 'Ratio', 'Ср. скорость (всё время)', 'Скорость за 1ч', 'Скорость за 24ч', 'МБ/ч', 'МБ/день', 'Ratio/ч (всего)', 'Ratio/ч (1ч)', 'Ratio/ч (24ч)', 'Дата создания'])
    for s in items:
        sz = int(s.get('size', 0)) if isinstance(s.get('size'), (int, float)) else 0
        up = int(s.get('uploaded', 0)) if isinstance(s.get('uploaded'), (int, float)) else 0
        rt = float(s.get('ratio', 0.0)) if isinstance(s.get('ratio'), (int, float)) else 0.0
        writer.writerow([
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('name', '')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('hash_id', '')),
            sz, f"{sz / (1024 ** 3):.2f}", up, f"{up / (1024 ** 3):.2f}", f"{rt:.2f}",
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_all_time_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_1h_fmt', '0 B/s')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('speed_24h_fmt', '0 B/s')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_hour_fmt', '0.00 МБ/ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('vol_day_fmt', '0.00 МБ/день')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_all_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_hour_fmt', '0.000 /ч')), sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('ratio_h_day_fmt', '0.000 /ч')),
            sanitize_csv_cell(s.get('created', ''))
        ])
    filename = f"summary_manual_{datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.csv"
    return Response(output.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename={filename}"})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/can_open_folder')

    if g.get('role') != 'admin':

    return jsonify({'can_open': _is_strict_localhost() and os.name == 'nt'})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/open_folder', methods=['POST'])
def api_logs_open_folder():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin' or not _is_strict_localhost() or os.name != 'nt':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Доступно только локально с localhost (не в Docker)'}), 403
    try:
        os.startfile(LOG_FOLDER)
        return jsonify({'success': True})
    except Exception as e:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': str(e)}), 500

    return jsonify({'days': summary_tracker.db.get_available_snapshot_days(limit=90)})


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/day')
def api_logs_by_day():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    date_str, start_ts, end_ts = _parse_day_bounds(request.args.get('date', ''))
    page = request.args.get('page', 1, type=int)
    per_page = request.args.get('per_page', 15, type=int)
    res = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_by_day_paginated(start_ts, end_ts, page=page, per_page=per_page)
    for it in res['items']:
        it['uploaded_fmt'] = format_file_size(it.get('total_uploaded', 0))
        it['size_fmt'] = format_file_size(it.get('total_size', 0))
    res['date'] = date_str
    return jsonify(res)


@analytics_bp.route('/api/logs/day/download')
def api_logs_by_day_download():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return "Только для Admin", 403
    date_str, start_ts, end_ts = _parse_day_bounds(request.args.get('date', ''))
    rows = summary_tracker.db.get_snapshots_by_day_all(start_ts, end_ts)
    output = io.StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(output, delimiter=';')
    writer.writerow(['Время снимка', 'Всего отдано (байт)', 'Всего отдано', 'Общий размер (байт)', 'Раздач'])
    for r in rows:
        writer.writerow([sanitize_csv_cell(r.get('datetime', '')), r.get('total_uploaded', 0), sanitize_csv_cell(format_file_size(r.get('total_uploaded', 0))), r.get('total_size', 0), r.get('count', 0)])
    return Response(output.getvalue().encode('utf-8-sig'), mimetype="text/csv", headers={"Content-Disposition": f"attachment; filename=snapshots_{date_str}.csv"})

# web/routes/automation_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify

from utils.config import cfg
from core.automation import automation_engine, VALID_TIME_MODES, VALID_SPEED_OPS

automation_bp = Blueprint('automation_bp', __name__)


def _parse_rule_payload(data):
    name = str(data.get('name', '')).strip()[:64] or 'Правило'
    time_mode = str(data.get('time_mode', 'seeding_time')).strip()
    if time_mode not in VALID_TIME_MODES:
        time_mode = 'seeding_time'

    try:
        min_ratio = max(0.0, float(data.get('min_ratio') or 0))
        min_days = max(0.0, float(data.get('min_seed_days') or 0))
        min_up_bytes = max(0, int(data.get('min_uploaded_bytes') or 0))
        speed_op = str(data.get('speed_op', '') or '').strip()
        if speed_op not in VALID_SPEED_OPS:
            speed_op = ''
        speed_bytes = max(0.0, float(data.get('speed_bytes') or 0))
        speed_win = max(1, min(10080, int(data.get('speed_window_min') or 60)))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return None, 'Некорректные числовые параметры правила'

    use_speed = (speed_op in ('>=', '<') and speed_bytes > 0)
    if min_ratio <= 0 and min_days <= 0 and min_up_bytes <= 0 and not use_speed:
        return None, 'Укажите хотя бы одно условие (Ratio, Время, Отдано или Среднюю скорость)'

    return {
        'name': name, 'time_mode': time_mode, 'min_ratio': min_ratio,
        'min_seed_days': min_days, 'min_uploaded_bytes': min_up_bytes,
        'speed_op': speed_op if use_speed else '', 'speed_bytes': speed_bytes if use_speed else 0.0,
        'speed_window_min': speed_win, 'action_pause': bool(data.get('action_pause', False)),
        'action_tag': str(data.get('action_tag', '')).strip()[:32],
        'action_remove_tag': str(data.get('action_remove_tag', '')).strip()[:32],
        'notify': bool(data.get('notify', True))
    }, None


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation', methods=['GET'])
def api_automation_get():
    return jsonify({
        'settings': {
            'enabled': cfg.getboolean('Auto_Enabled', False),
            'interval': cfg.getint('Auto_Interval_Min', 5),
            'has_tg_token': bool(cfg.get('TG_Bot_Token', '').strip()),
            'tg_chat_id': cfg.get('TG_Chat_ID', ''),
            'has_discord': bool(cfg.get('Discord_Webhook', '').strip()),
            'alert_disk_gib': cfg.getint('Alert_Disk_Min_GiB', 0),
            'alert_qb_down': cfg.getboolean('Alert_QB_Down', False)
        },
        'rules': automation_engine.get_rules()
    })


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/settings', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_settings():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    enabled = bool(data.get('enabled', False))
    try:
        interval = max(1, min(1440, int(data.get('interval', 5))))
        alert_disk = max(0, min(100000, int(data.get('alert_disk_gib', 0) or 0)))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные числовые параметры'}), 400

    tg_token = str(data.get('tg_token', '')).strip()
    tg_chat = str(data.get('tg_chat_id', '')).strip()
    discord = str(data.get('discord_webhook', '')).strip()

    if discord and not (discord.startswith('https://discord.com/api/webhooks/') or discord.startswith('https://discordapp.com/api/webhooks/')):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный URL Discord Webhook!'}), 400

    cfg.set('Auto_Enabled', str(enabled))
    cfg.set('Auto_Interval_Min', str(interval))
    cfg.set('Alert_Disk_Min_GiB', str(alert_disk))
    cfg.set('Alert_QB_Down', str(bool(data.get('alert_qb_down', False))))
    if tg_token:
        cfg.set('TG_Bot_Token', tg_token)
    cfg.set('TG_Chat_ID', tg_chat)
    if discord:
        cfg.set('Discord_Webhook', discord)
    cfg.save()

    return jsonify({'success': True, 'message': 'Настройки автопилота сохранены!'})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/test', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_test():
    ok, results = automation_engine.test_notifications()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': ' | '.join(results)})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/run_now', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_run_now():
    cnt = automation_engine.run_cycle()
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'triggered': cnt, 'message': f'Проверка завершена. Сработало раздач: {cnt}'})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_add_rule():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    parsed, err = _parse_rule_payload(data)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    rid = automation_engine.add_rule(**parsed)
    return jsonify({'success': bool(rid), 'id': rid})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/update', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_update_rule():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    rid = data.get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID правила'}), 400
    try:
        rid_int = int(rid)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректный ID правила'}), 400
    parsed, err = _parse_rule_payload(data)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    ok = automation_engine.update_rule(rid_int, **parsed)
    return jsonify({'success': ok}), (200 if ok else 404)


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/toggle', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_toggle_rule():
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': automation_engine.toggle_rule(rid)})


@automation_bp.route('/api/automation/rules/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_automation_delete_rule():
    rid = (request.get_json(silent=True) or {}).get('id')
    if not rid:
        return jsonify({'success': False}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': automation_engine.delete_rule(rid)})

# web/routes/profiles_bp.py
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, g
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.qb_api import get_local_ips
from web.security import check_rate_limit, record_failed_login

profiles_bp = Blueprint('profiles_bp', __name__)


@profiles_bp.route('/api/me')
def api_me():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'authenticated': False, 'role': 'viewer', 'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(0)})
    return jsonify({
        'authenticated': True,
        'id': prof['id'],
        'name': prof['name'],
        'login': prof.get('login', ''),
        'role': prof['role'],
        'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(prof['id'])
    })


@profiles_bp.route('/api/auth/exchange', methods=['POST'])
def api_auth_exchange():
    ip = (request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0').split(',')[0].strip()
    if not check_rate_limit(ip):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Слишком много попыток. Подождите 5 минут.'}), 429

    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    login = str(data.get('login', '')).strip()
    password = str(data.get('password', '')).strip()
    prof = profiles_db.authenticate_login(login, password)
    if not prof:
        record_failed_login(ip)
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неверный логин или пароль'}), 401

    return jsonify({
        'success': True,
        'api_token': prof.get('api_token', ''),
        'role': prof['role'],
        'name': prof['name']
    })


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profile/ui', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_profile_ui():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    pid = prof['id'] if prof else 0
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({'ui_settings': profiles_db.get_ui_settings(pid)})
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    saved = profiles_db.save_ui_settings(pid, data)
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'ui_settings': saved})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profile/password', methods=['POST'])
def api_self_password():
    prof = g.get('profile')
    if not prof:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не авторизован'}), 401
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    new_pwd = str(data.get('password', '')).strip()
    tok, err = profiles_db.change_password(prof['id'], new_password=new_pwd)
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/network/hosts')
def api_network_hosts():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ips = get_local_ips()
    return jsonify({'ips': ips})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles')
def api_profiles_list():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    return jsonify({'profiles': profiles_db.list_profiles()})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/create', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_create():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid, raw_pwd, api_tok, err = profiles_db.create_profile(
        data.get('name', ''), data.get('role', 'viewer'),
        login=data.get('login', ''), password=data.get('password', '')
    )
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err or 'Не удалось создать профиль'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'id': pid, 'password': raw_pwd, 'api_token': api_tok})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/change_password', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_change_password():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    new_pwd, err = profiles_db.change_password(pid, new_password=data.get('password', ''), new_login=data.get('login'))
    if err:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': err}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'password': new_pwd})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/invite', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_invite():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    inv = profiles_db.create_invite(pid)
    if not inv:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не удалось создать инвайт'}), 400
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'invite_token': inv})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/reissue_api', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_reissue_api():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    tok = profiles_db.reissue_api_token(pid)
    if not tok:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Профиль не найден'}), 404
    return jsonify({'success': True, 'api_token': tok})


@profiles_bp.route('/api/profiles/delete', methods=['POST'])
def api_profiles_delete():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    pid = data.get('id')
    if not pid:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Не указан ID'}), 400
    ok, msg = profiles_db.delete_profile(pid)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg, 'error': None if ok else msg}), (200 if ok else 400)

# web/routes/settings_bp.py
import hmac
import datetime
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, render_template, redirect, session, current_app, Response, g

from utils.config import cfg
from core.qb_api import reset_qb_session
from core.autostart import is_autostart_enabled
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.scheduler import ALLOWED_INTERVALS, normalize_interval
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.backup_manager import create_backup_zip, stage_restore_zip
from web.security import get_csrf_token, is_allowed_qb_ip, check_rate_limit, record_failed_login

settings_bp = Blueprint('settings_bp', __name__)


@settings_bp.route('/invite/<token>', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def invite_accept(token):
    inv = profiles_db.get_valid_invite(token)
    if not inv:
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Инвайт-ссылка недействительна, просрочена или уже использована.', invite=None), 410
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=None, invite=inv)
    expected, sent = session.get('csrf_token'), request.form.get('csrf_token')
    if not (expected and sent and hmac.compare_digest(str(expected), str(sent))):
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='CSRF ошибка, обновите страницу.', invite=inv), 403
    prof, err = profiles_db.consume_invite(token, request.form.get('login', '').strip(), request.form.get('password', '').strip())
    if err or not prof:
        return render_template('invite.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=err or 'Ошибка активации', invite=inv), 400
    session['authenticated'] = True
    session['profile_id'] = prof['id']
    return redirect('/')


@settings_bp.route('/login', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def login():
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error=None)
    ip = (request.headers.get('X-Forwarded-For') or request.remote_addr or '0.0.0.0').split(',')[0].strip()
    if not check_rate_limit(ip):
        return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Слишком много попыток входа. Подождите 5 минут.'), 429

    login_val, pwd = request.form.get('login', '').strip(), request.form.get('password', '').strip()
    real_pwd = cfg.get('Web_Password', '')
    if real_pwd and hmac.compare_digest(pwd, real_pwd) and (not login_val or login_val.lower() == 'admin'):
        session['authenticated'] = True
        adm = profiles_db.get_default_admin()
        if adm:
            session['profile_id'] = adm['id']
        return redirect('/')

    prof = profiles_db.authenticate_login(login_val, pwd) if login_val else profiles_db.authenticate_api_token(pwd)
    if prof:
        session['authenticated'] = True
        session['profile_id'] = prof['id']
        return redirect('/')

    record_failed_login(ip)
    return render_template('login.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), error='Неверный логин или пароль!'), 401


@settings_bp.route('/logout', methods=['POST', 'GET'])
def logout():
    session.pop('authenticated', None)
    session.pop('profile_id', None)
    return redirect('/login')


@settings_bp.route('/')
def index():
    return render_template('index.html', csrf_token=get_csrf_token(), auth_enabled=current_app.config.get('IS_EXPOSED', False), v=current_app.config.get('BUILD_VER', '1'))


@settings_bp.route('/api/backup/export')
def api_backup_export():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    ex_hist = request.args.get('exclude_history', '0') == '1'
    strip_sec = request.args.get('strip_secrets', '0') == '1'
    zip_bytes = create_backup_zip(exclude_history=ex_hist, strip_secrets=strip_sec)
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    return Response(zip_bytes, mimetype='application/zip', headers={'Content-Disposition': f'attachment; filename=tbr_backup_{ts}.zip'})


@settings_bp.route('/api/backup/restore', methods=['POST'])
def api_backup_restore():
    if g.get('role') != 'admin':
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Только для Admin'}), 403
    f = request.files.get('file')
    if not f:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Файл не передан'}), 400
    ok, msg = stage_restore_zip(f.read())
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg}), (200 if ok else 400)


@settings_bp.route('/api/settings', methods=['GET', 'POST'])
def api_settings():
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return jsonify({
            'web_port': cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000), 'web_host': cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1'),
            'has_password': bool(cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip()),
            'qb_ip': cfg.get('WebUI_Address', ''), 'qb_port': cfg.get('WebUI_Port', ''),
            'qb_user': cfg.get('WebUI_User', 'admin'), 'has_qb_password': bool(cfg.get('WebUI_Password', '').strip()),
            'w1': cfg.getint('Weight_W1', 70), 'w2': cfg.getint('Weight_W2', 30), 'w3': cfg.getint('Weight_W3', 0),
            'snapshot_interval': normalize_interval(cfg.getint('Snapshot_Interval_Min', 60)),
            'autostart': is_autostart_enabled()
        })

    data = request.get_json(silent=True)
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные данные'}), 400

    old_p, old_h = cfg.getint('Web_Port', 5000), cfg.get('Web_Host', '127.0.0.1').strip() or '127.0.0.1'
    try:
        new_p = int(data.get('web_port', old_p))
        if not (1 <= new_p <= 65535):
            raise ValueError
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Порт должен быть числом от 1 до 65535'}), 400

    qb_ip = str(data.get('qb_ip', '')).strip()
    if not is_allowed_qb_ip(qb_ip):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Недопустимый IP qBittorrent (разрешены только локальные сети и NetBird)'}), 400

    qb_port_raw = str(data.get('qb_port', '')).strip()
    if qb_port_raw:
        try:
            if not (1 <= int(qb_port_raw) <= 65535):
                raise ValueError
        except ValueError:
            return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Порт qBittorrent должен быть от 1 до 65535'}), 400

    try:
        w1, w2, w3 = int(data.get('w1', 70)), int(data.get('w2', 30)), int(data.get('w3', 0))
        if w1 < 0 or w2 < 0 or w3 < 0 or (w1 + w2 + w3) <= 0:
            raise ValueError
        snap_iv = int(data.get('snapshot_interval', 60))
        if snap_iv not in ALLOWED_INTERVALS:
            raise ValueError
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные веса или интервал замеров'}), 400

    new_host = str(data.get('web_host', '127.0.0.1')).strip() or '127.0.0.1'
    new_pass = str(data.get('web_password', '')).strip()
    if new_host not in ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost') and not (new_pass or cfg.get('Web_Password', '').strip()):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Для внешнего хоста обязательно задайте пароль!'}), 400

    old_snap_iv = normalize_interval(cfg.getint('Snapshot_Interval_Min', 60))
    cfg.set('Web_Port', new_p); cfg.set('Web_Host', new_host)
    if new_pass:
        cfg.set('Web_Password', new_pass)
    cfg.set('WebUI_Address', qb_ip); cfg.set('WebUI_Port', qb_port_raw)
    cfg.set('WebUI_User', str(data.get('qb_user', 'admin')).strip() or 'admin')
    if str(data.get('qb_password', '')):
        cfg.set('WebUI_Password', str(data.get('qb_password', '')))
    cfg.set('Weight_W1', w1); cfg.set('Weight_W2', w2); cfg.set('Weight_W3', w3)
    cfg.set('Snapshot_Interval_Min', snap_iv); cfg.save()

    reset_qb_session()
    if old_snap_iv != snap_iv:
        summary_tracker.notify_interval_changed()

    net_changed = (old_p != new_p) or (old_h != new_host)
    if net_changed:
        ws = current_app.config.get('WEB_SERVER')
        if ws:
            ws.restart(new_host, new_p)

    return jsonify({'success': True, 'port_changed': net_changed, 'new_port': new_p, 'message': 'Настройки сохранены! Веб-сервер перезапущен.' if net_changed else 'Настройки сохранены!'})

# web/routes/torrents_bp.py
import time
import urllib.parse
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify

from utils.config import cfg
from utils.helpers import format_file_size
from core.torrent_parser import get_all_torrents, force_clear_cache
from core.qb_api import (
    detect_qbittorrent_port, get_live_torrent_info, send_qb_command,
    add_torrent_tags, remove_torrent_tags,
    is_qb_running, close_qbittorrent, launch_qbittorrent, scan_dead_torrents
)
from core.autostart import set_autostart, is_autostart_enabled
from core.fastresume_merger import run_fastresume_merge, execute_source_deletion, VALID_DELETE_MODES
from web.security import HASH_RE

torrents_bp = Blueprint('torrents_bp', __name__)


@torrents_bp.route('/api/qb/status')
def api_qb_status():
    running = is_qb_running()
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    return jsonify({'running': running, 'ip': ip, 'port': port})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/qb/start', methods=['POST'])
def api_qb_start():
    ok = launch_qbittorrent()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': 'qBittorrent запущен' if ok else 'Не удалось запустить'})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/qb/stop', methods=['POST'])
def api_qb_stop():
    ok = close_qbittorrent()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': 'qBittorrent остановлен' if ok else 'Не удалось остановить'})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/qb/restart', methods=['POST'])
def api_qb_restart():
    close_qbittorrent()
    time.sleep(1.5)
    ok = launch_qbittorrent()
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': 'qBittorrent перезапущен'})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/autostart/status')
def api_autostart_status():
    return jsonify({'enabled': is_autostart_enabled()})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/autostart/toggle', methods=['POST'])
def api_autostart_toggle():
    curr = is_autostart_enabled()
    ok, msg = set_autostart(not curr)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'enabled': not curr if ok else curr, 'message': msg})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents')
def api_torrents():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    data_dict = get_all_torrents(use_cache=True)
    live_info = get_live_torrent_info(ip, port) if port else {}

    result = []
    for t_id, static in data_dict.items():
        live = live_info.get(t_id, {})
        size = live.get('total_size', static.get('size', 1))
        uploaded = live.get('uploaded', static.get('uploaded', 0))
        ratio = round(uploaded / size, 4) if size > 0 else 0.0
        state = live.get('state', 'seeding' if static.get('active_seconds', 0) > 0 else 'unknown')
        save_path = live.get('save_path', static.get('save_path', ''))
        tags = str(live.get('tags', '') or '')

        # Домен трекера для фильтрации
        tracker_host = ''
        tr_url = str(live.get('tracker', '') or '')
        if not tr_url and static.get('trackers'):
            tr_url = str(static['trackers'][0] or '')
        if tr_url:
            tracker_host = urllib.parse.urlparse(tr_url).netloc.lower()

        name = static.get('name', '')
        if not name or name.lower() == t_id.lower() or len(name) in (40, 64):
            if live.get('name'):
                name = live['name']
        if not name:
            name = t_id

        result.append({
            'hash': t_id, 'name': name, 'size': size, 'size_fmt': format_file_size(size),
            'uploaded': uploaded, 'uploaded_fmt': format_file_size(uploaded),
            'ratio': ratio, 'state': state, 'save_path': save_path,
            'tags': tags, 'tracker': tracker_host,
            'active_seconds': static.get('active_seconds', 0)
        })
    result.sort(key=lambda x: str(x['name']).lower())
    return jsonify({'torrents': result, 'qb_connected': bool(port), 'qb_running': is_qb_running()})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/refresh', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_refresh():
    force_clear_cache()
    return jsonify({'status': 'ok'})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/scan_dead', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_scan_dead():
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400
    res = scan_dead_torrents(ip, port)
    if res.get('success'):
        for item in res.get('items', []):
            item['size_fmt'] = format_file_size(item.get('size', 0))
    return jsonify(res)


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/action', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrent_action():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    action = data.get('action')
    t_hash = data.get('hash', '')
    tag = str(data.get('tag', '')).strip()
    if action not in ('stop', 'start', 'setForceStart', 'addTags', 'removeTags'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неизвестная команда'}), 400

    hashes = [h.strip() for h in str(t_hash).split('|') if h.strip()]
    if not hashes or not all(HASH_RE.fullmatch(h) for h in hashes):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Некорректные хэши'}), 400

    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent недоступен'}), 400

    joined = '|'.join(hashes)
    if action == 'addTags':
        return jsonify({'success': add_torrent_tags(ip, port, joined, tag)})
    if action == 'removeTags':
        return jsonify({'success': remove_torrent_tags(ip, port, joined, tag)})
    return jsonify({'success': send_qb_command(ip, port, action, joined)})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/torrents/action_all', methods=['POST'])
def api_torrents_action_all():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    action = data.get('action', 'stop')
    if action not in ('stop', 'start'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'Неизвестная команда'}), 400
    ip, port = detect_qbittorrent_port(cfg.config)
    if not port:
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'error': 'qBittorrent не запущен'}), 400
    ok = send_qb_command(ip, port, action, 'all')
    msg = "Все раздачи поставлены на паузу" if action == 'stop' else "Все раздачи возобновлены"
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'message': msg})


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/run', methods=['POST'])
def api_merger_run():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    old_hash = str(data.get('old_hash', '')).strip()
    new_hash = str(data.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    delete_mode = str(data.get('delete_mode', 'none')).strip()
    if delete_mode not in VALID_DELETE_MODES:
        delete_mode = 'none'

    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Некорректные или одинаковые хэши торрентов!']}), 400

    ok, log, confirm_info, code = run_fastresume_merge(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode=delete_mode)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'log': log, 'needs_delete_confirm': bool(ok and confirm_info), 'confirm_info': confirm_info}), code


@torrents_bp.route('/api/merger/delete_source', methods=['POST'])
def api_merger_delete_source():
    data = request.get_json(silent=True) or {}
    old_hash, new_hash = str(data.get('old_hash', '')).strip(), str(data.get('new_hash', '')).strip()
    delete_mode, confirmed = str(data.get('delete_mode', '')).strip(), bool(data.get('confirmed', False))

    if not confirmed or delete_mode not in ('safe', 'force'):
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Удаление не подтверждено!']}), 400
    if not HASH_RE.fullmatch(old_hash) or not HASH_RE.fullmatch(new_hash) or old_hash.lower() == new_hash.lower():
        return jsonify({'success': False, 'log': ['❌ Некорректные или одинаковые хэши!']}), 400

    ok, log = execute_source_deletion(old_hash, new_hash, delete_mode)
    return jsonify({'success': ok, 'log': log}), (200 if ok else 400)

# web/routes/widget_bp.py
import io
import json
from PIL import Image
from flask import Blueprint, request, jsonify, Response, g
from core.summary_tracker import summary_tracker
from core.profiles_db import profiles_db
from core.widget_service import get_widget_summary, render_widget_chart_png, get_pwa_icon_png

widget_bp = Blueprint('widget_bp', __name__)
_FAVICON_CACHE = None


@widget_bp.route('/favicon.ico')
def favicon_ico():
    global _FAVICON_CACHE
    if _FAVICON_CACHE is None:
        img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(get_pwa_icon_png(64)))
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        img.save(buf, format='ICO', sizes=[(32, 32), (64, 64)])
        _FAVICON_CACHE = buf.getvalue()
    return Response(_FAVICON_CACHE, mimetype='image/x-icon', headers={'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=86400'})

def pwa_manifest():
    manifest = {
        "name": "tbr PRO — qBittorrent",
        "short_name": "tbr PRO",
        "start_url": "/",
        "display": "standalone",
        "background_color": "#121214",
        "theme_color": "#1a1a20",
        "icons": [
            {"src": "/icon-192.png", "sizes": "192x192", "type": "image/png", "purpose": "any maskable"},
            {"src": "/icon-512.png", "sizes": "512x512", "type": "image/png", "purpose": "any maskable"}
        ]
    }
    return Response(json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False), mimetype='application/manifest+json', headers={'Cache-Control': 'no-store'})
